   IDEA a pocket guideline card set, every CQ a card, coloured by its GRADE like a printed evidence table
   PICTURE the evidence map: all 96 CQs as tiles in 18 domain rows, fill = certainty, shape = strength

An acute flare of chronic pancreatitis can also meet these criteria, so underlying chronic disease should be kept in mind.&quot;,&quot;The enzyme cut-off of three times the upper limit of normal is imperfect. centerへの転送が推奨される。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:null,&quot;quality&quot;:null}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;病状は急速に進行しうる一方、転送自体にもリスクがあるため、転送の判断は慎重に行う。転送は予防的転送と反応的転送に分けて考えるとよい。&quot;,&quot;予防的転送は、持続SIRSなど重症化が予測され、自施設の設備や専門性が不足していて悪化リスクが高い患者で考慮する。初期治療に反応しない患者は、多職種の専門性を持つ三次施設への転送を検討する。&quot;,&quot;反応的転送は、臓器不全や、自施設では行えない侵襲的治療を要する感染性膵壊死などの合併症が生じた場合に考慮する。米国のデータではhigh-volume center（年間118例以上）の死亡リスクは25%低かった。地域の三次・二次施設のネットワーク構築が適切な判断を助ける。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;The disease can progress quickly, yet transfer carries its own risk, so the decision must be careful. Transfer can be thought of as prophylactic or reactive.&quot;,&quot;Prophylactic transfer suits patients predicted to have severe AP, for example with persistent SIRS, at high risk of deterioration where local resources or expertise are lacking. Patients who do not respond to initial treatment should be considered for a tertiary center with multidisciplinary expertise.&quot;,&quot;Reactive transfer is for organ failure or complications such as infected necrosis needing interventions unavailable locally. In US data, high-volume centers (118 or more cases per year) had a 25 % lower risk of death. Regional networks of tertiary and secondary centers can support these decisions.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;US Nationwide Inpatient Sample: AP patients at high-volume centers (&gt;=118 cases/year) had a 25 % lower risk of death&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;米国Nationwide Inpatient Sample：high-volume center（年間118例以上）では死亡リスクが25%低い&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Large national study: older age, male sex, lower income, non-teaching hospital, gallstone pancreatitis, need for surgical intervention and severe AP predicted transfer to large centers&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;大規模全国研究：高齢、男性、低所得、非教育病院、胆石性膵炎、外科的介入の適応、重症急性膵炎が大規模施設への転送の予測因子&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;予後スコアは転送判断の参考になる。地域の多職種ネットワークの整備が望ましい。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;Prognostic scores can help the decision. Establishing regional multidisciplinary networks is encouraged.&quot;,&quot;patients_ja&quot;:&quot;患者は重症化が予測された場合に最善の医療を受けられる保証を求めている。早期に飲食できるかどうかにも関心が高く、高トリグリセリド血症関連の膵炎では必要以上に絶食が続いたという経験もある。施設間の早期かつ繰り返しの連携による確実な転送体制と、その受け入れ能力の確保を求めている。&quot;,&quot;patients_en&quot;:&quot;Patients want assurance of the best possible care when severe AP is predicted. They ask for early, repeated liaison between centers so transfer happens reliably, and for enough capacity in the system.&quot;},{&quot;q&quot;:7,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;III&quot;,&quot;question_en&quot;:&quot;What is the indication and timing of the initial CT assessment in AP?&quot;,&quot;question_ja&quot;:&quot;急性膵炎における初回CT評価の適応とタイミングは何か&quot;,&quot;statements&quot;:[{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;rec&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;Initial CT assessment in AP should be done if there is (i) diagnostic uncertainty and (ii) failure to respond to conservative treatment or clinical deterioration. The optimal timing for initial conventional CT to assess severity is at least 72-96 hours after the onset of symptoms.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;急性膵炎の初回CT評価は、(i) 診断が不確実な場合、(ii) 保存的治療に反応しない場合または臨床的に悪化した場合に行うべきである。重症度評価のための初回の通常CTの至適時期は、症状発現から少なくとも72〜96時間後である。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:&quot;strong&quot;,&quot;quality&quot;:&quot;moderate&quot;}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;造影CTは診断と重症度予測に正確であるが、48時間以内の早期ルーチンCTは推奨されない。理由は3つある。早期CTが臨床転帰を改善する根拠がない、CTスコアは臨床スコアより重症度・予後予測に優れない、膵および膵周囲壊死の全容は発症72〜96時間以降でないと明らかにならない。&quot;,&quot;したがってCTSIや改訂CTSIによる重症度評価のCTはこの時期以降に行う。&quot;,&quot;一方、急性腹症で診断が不確かな場合は、腸管虚血、急性胆嚢炎、外傷性膵損傷、癌関連膵炎、血管緊急症、腸閉塞や穿孔の除外のために早期CTが役立つ。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;Contrast-enhanced CT is accurate for diagnosis and severity, but routine CT within 48 hours is not recommended for three reasons. Early CT has not been shown to improve outcomes, CT scores are not better than clinical scores for prognosis, and the full extent of necrosis appears only after 72 to 96 hours.&quot;,&quot;CT for severity grading with CTSI or modified CTSI should therefore be done after that window.&quot;,&quot;Early CT remains useful when the diagnosis is uncertain in an acute abdomen, to exclude bowel ischemia, acute cholecystitis, traumatic pancreatic injury, cancer-related pancreatitis, vascular emergencies, or bowel obstruction and perforation.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;No evidence that early CT improves clinical outcome or that early detection of necrosis changes treatment&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;早期CTが臨床転帰を改善する、または壊死の早期検出が治療を変えるという根拠はない&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;CT scoring systems are not superior to clinical scoring systems for predicting prognosis and severity&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;CTスコアは予後・重症度予測において臨床スコアに優らない&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;入院時の「とりあえずCT」は避ける。重症度評価目的のCTは発症72〜96時間以降に撮る。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;Avoid reflex CT at admission. Most complications can be suspected clinically, so CECT is reserved for when intervention is considered or the patient fails to improve.&quot;,&quot;Arterial pseudoaneurysm may stay silent until it bleeds, but it is uncommon and does not justify routine scanning.&quot;,&quot;CT is poor at detecting necrosis within fluid-predominant collections, and MRI 3 to 4 weeks after onset may be needed to separate pseudocyst from walled-off necrosis. MRI also helps with contrast allergy or renal failure, since T2-weighted images show necrosis without gadolinium. Perfusion or dual-energy CT may predict necrosis early, but its effect on outcomes is unknown.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Routine (e.g., weekly) follow-up CT lacks supporting evidence&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;毎週などの定期的フォローアップCTを支持する根拠はない&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;MRI at least 3-4 weeks after onset may be required to distinguish pseudocyst from walled-off necrosis&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;仮性嚢胞と被包化壊死の鑑別には発症3〜4週以降のMRIが必要なことがある&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;フォローアップ画像は「治療方針を変えるとき」に撮る。被曝や造影剤の負担も考慮する。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;Repeat imaging when it may change management. Consider radiation and contrast burden.&quot;,&quot;patients_ja&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;patients_en&quot;:&quot;&quot;},{&quot;q&quot;:9,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;III&quot;,&quot;question_en&quot;:&quot;What is the optimal CT and MR protocol to detect local complications?&quot;,&quot;question_ja&quot;:&quot;局所合併症を検出するための最適なCTおよびMRのプロトコルは何か&quot;,&quot;statements&quot;:[{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;gps&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;To detect local complications, it is recommended to perform a multidetector CT with thin collimation and slice thickness (i.e., 2 mm or less) during the pancreatic and portal venous phases (i.e., 50-70 s delay) after giving 100-150 ml of non-ionic intravenous contrast material at a rate of 3 ml/s. Only a portal venous phase (mono-phasic) CT is generally sufficient to assess fluid collections with or without necrosis during follow-up. If a pseudoaneurysm is suspected, dynamic CT or arterial phase contrast CT should be added.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;局所合併症の検出には、非イオン性造影剤100〜150 mlを3 ml/sで静注した後、膵実質相と門脈相（遅延50〜70秒）で、薄いコリメーションとスライス厚（2 mm以下）のマルチディテクターCTを撮影することが推奨される。経過観察で壊死の有無を問わず液体貯留を評価するには、通常は門脈相のみ（単相）のCTで十分である。仮性動脈瘤が疑われる場合はダイナミックCTまたは動脈相造影CTを追加すべきである。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:null,&quot;quality&quot;:null},{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;gps&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;For MRI, the recommendation is to perform axial FS-T2W and FS-T1W scanning, intravenous gadolinium contrast may be required if the MRI is being done instead of a contrast-enhanced CT. If an MRI is done to delineate necrotic and liquefied components of fluid collections, an axial FS-T2W sequence should suffice.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;MRIでは、軸位断の脂肪抑制T2強調像および脂肪抑制T1強調像の撮影が推奨され、造影CTの代わりにMRIを行う場合はガドリニウム造影剤の静注が必要となることがある。液体貯留内の壊死成分と液状成分の描出を目的とする場合は、軸位断の脂肪抑制T2強調像で十分である。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:null,&quot;quality&quot;:null}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;CTとMRIの撮像法は文献によりばらつきが大きく、専用の放射線学的ガイドラインはない。膵実質相と門脈相はいずれも生存膵と非生存膵の区別に十分である。&quot;,&quot;出血、仮性動脈瘤、腸間膜虚血が疑われる場合は多相撮影が必要となる。膵壊死は造影CTで1 cm²超の非造影域で、CT値が30〜50 HU未満または造影による上昇が15 HU未満の領域とされる。急性腎障害では単純CTも選択肢となる。&quot;,&quot;T2強調MRIは、仮性嚢胞と壊死を含む貯留（急性壊死性貯留、WON）の区別やドレナージ前の壊死量の評価が必要な場合、また被曝を避けたい若年患者で勧められる。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;CT and MRI protocols vary widely and no dedicated radiology guideline exists. Both pancreatic and portal venous phases are adequate to separate viable from non-viable tissue.&quot;,&quot;A multiphase protocol is needed for suspected hemorrhage, pseudoaneurysm or mesenteric ischemia. Necrosis is defined as a non-enhancing area over 1 cm² with attenuation below 30 to 50 HU or an increase below 15 HU after contrast. Non-contrast CT is an option in acute renal failure.&quot;,&quot;T2-weighted MRI is advised when pseudocysts must be separated from necrotic collections or necrosis quantified before drainage, and in young patients because of radiation.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Pancreatic necrosis on CECT: non-enhancing area &gt;1 cm² with CT value &lt;30-50 HU or &lt;15 HU increase after contrast&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;造影CTでの膵壊死：1 cm²超の非造影域で、CT値30〜50 HU未満または造影後の上昇15 HU未満&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Multiphase protocol indications: hemorrhage, arterial pseudoaneurysm, mesenteric ischemia&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;多相撮影の適応：出血、動脈性仮性動脈瘤、腸間膜虚血&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;経過観察は門脈相の単相CTで十分なことが多く、被曝を抑えられる。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;Follow-up can usually use a single portal venous phase, which limits radiation.&quot;,&quot;patients_ja&quot;:&quot;患者は初回CTのタイミングについて明確な説明を求めている。診断目的なら入院当日にCTを撮ることがあるが、重症度評価には発症後少なくとも72〜96時間を要し、臨床的悪化時には後で追加撮影が必要となりうる点を伝えるべきとしている。&quot;,&quot;patients_en&quot;:&quot;Patients want the timing of the first CT explained. A scan for diagnosis may be done on the day of admission, but severity assessment needs at least 72 to 96 hours after onset, and further scans may be needed if they deteriorate.&quot;},{&quot;q&quot;:10,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;IV&quot;,&quot;question_en&quot;:&quot;What is the preferred fluid type to use for fluid therapy in AP?&quot;,&quot;question_ja&quot;:&quot;急性膵炎の輸液療法に用いるべき輸液の種類は何か&quot;,&quot;statements&quot;:[{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;rec&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;Lactated Ringer&#x27;s solution should be used for fluid therapy in patients with AP.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;急性膵炎患者の輸液療法には乳酸リンゲル液を用いるべきである。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:&quot;strong&quot;,&quot;quality&quot;:&quot;moderate&quot;}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;乳酸リンゲル液（LR）と生理食塩液を比較したRCTは6本あるが、多くは単施設の小規模試験である。&quot;,&quot;複数のメタアナリシスで、LRは重症度、ICU入室、局所合併症の低下と関連していた。個々の試験の結果は一致せず、多くは臨床的に重要なエンドポイントを検出する検出力がなかったため、大規模多施設試験が望まれる。&quot;,&quot;最近のRCTでは、バランス型多電解質液は生理食塩液と比べて血中クロール値が低く、SIRSが少なかった。生理食塩液の大量投与による高クロール血症を避ける意味でも、晶質液はバランス型が基本である。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;Six fully published RCTs compared lactated Ringer&#x27;s with normal saline, most of them small and single-center.&quot;,&quot;Several meta-analyses link LR with lower severity, fewer ICU admissions and fewer local complications. Individual trial results conflicted and most lacked power for clinically relevant endpoints, so large multicenter trials are still needed.&quot;,&quot;A recent RCT found that a balanced multi-electrolyte solution gave lower chloride levels and less SIRS than saline, supporting balanced crystalloids as the default.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Six full-published RCTs of LR vs normal saline, mostly small single-center studies&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;LRと生理食塩液を比較した6本のRCT（多くは単施設小規模）&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Meta-analyses: LR associated with lower severity, ICU admission and local complications&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;メタアナリシス：LRは重症度、ICU入室、局所合併症の低下と関連&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;RCT of balanced multi-electrolyte solution vs normal saline: lower chloride levels and less SIRS with the balanced solution&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;バランス型多電解質液と生理食塩液のRCT：バランス型で血中クロール低値、SIRS減少&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;patients_ja&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;patients_en&quot;:&quot;&quot;},{&quot;q&quot;:11,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;IV&quot;,&quot;question_en&quot;:&quot;What is the optimal fluid infusion strategy for fluid therapy in AP?&quot;,&quot;question_ja&quot;:&quot;急性膵炎の輸液療法における最適な輸液戦略は何か&quot;,&quot;statements&quot;:[{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;rec&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;A moderate fluid infusion rate of 1.5 ml/kg/h is recommended. A fluid bolus is recommended if the patient has hypovolemia or hypotension at presentation. Additional fluids may be given depending on hematocrit and clinical signs of hypovolemia.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;1.5 ml/kg/hの中等量の輸液速度が推奨される。来院時に循環血液量減少または低血圧がある場合はボーラス輸液が推奨される。ヘマトクリットと循環血液量減少の臨床徴候に応じて追加輸液を行ってもよい。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:&quot;strong&quot;,&quot;quality&quot;:&quot;moderate&quot;}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;輸液速度を比較したRCTは6本ある。1993年アトランタ分類で重症とされた患者を対象とした2本では、積極的輸液のほうが転帰が悪かった。SIRS改善に差がなかった試験や、発症24時間以降の登録で差がなかった試験もある。軽症例（n=60）で積極的輸液が臨床改善を早めたというオープンラベル試験もある。&quot;,&quot;最も新しい国際多施設オープンラベルRCTは、重症化の基準を持たない全急性膵炎を対象に、積極的輸液（20 mL/kgボーラス後3 mL/kg/h）と中等量輸液（1.5 mL/kg/h、循環血液量減少時に10 mL/kgボーラス）を比較した。n=249で早期中止となり、体液過剰は21%対6%（p&lt;0.01）と積極的輸液群で多く、主要評価項目の中等症・重症への進展に有意差はなかった。&quot;,&quot;軽症では毛細血管の機能が保たれる一方、重症では毛細血管漏出が起こりうるため、重症予測例ではより慎重な輸液が必要である。積極的輸液は膵壊死や臓器不全を防がず、むしろ転帰悪化と関連する。&quot;,&quot;輸液は救命（ショック時のボーラス）、最適化（慎重に滴定）、安定化（維持のみ）、減量（体液過剰時の負のバランス）の4相で考えるとよい。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;Six RCTs have tested infusion rates. Two trials in severe AP defined by the 1993 Atlanta criteria found worse outcomes with aggressive fluids. Another showed no difference in SIRS reduction, and one enrolling after 24 hours found no difference. An open-label trial in mild AP (n = 60) found faster improvement with aggressive fluids.&quot;,&quot;The latest international multicenter open-label RCT compared aggressive fluids (20 mL/kg bolus then 3 mL/kg/h) with moderate fluids (1.5 mL/kg/h, 10 mL/kg bolus if hypovolemic) in all-comers without baseline severity criteria. It stopped early at n = 249 because fluid overload occurred in 21 % vs 6 % (p &lt; 0.01), with no significant difference in progression to moderate-severe AP.&quot;,&quot;Capillary function is preserved in mild AP, but severe AP may cause capillary leak, so fluids must be more guarded in predicted severe disease. Aggressive fluids do not prevent necrosis or organ failure and are linked to worse outcomes.&quot;,&quot;Fluid therapy can be framed in four phases: rescue with boluses in shock, cautious optimization, stabilization with maintenance only, and de-escalation with a negative balance if overloaded.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;International multicenter open-label RCT, stopped early (n = 249): aggressive (20 mL/kg bolus + 3 mL/kg/h) vs moderate (1.5 mL/kg/h) fluids, fluid overload 21 % vs 6 % (p &lt; 0.01), no significant difference in moderate-severe AP&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;国際多施設オープンラベルRCT、早期中止（n=249）：積極的輸液（20 mL/kgボーラス＋3 mL/kg/h）対中等量輸液（1.5 mL/kg/h）、体液過剰21%対6%（p&lt;0.01）、中等症・重症への進展に有意差なし&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Two RCTs in severe AP (1993 Atlanta definition): worse outcomes with aggressive fluid therapy&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;重症急性膵炎（1993年アトランタ分類）を対象とした2本のRCT：積極的輸液で転帰悪化&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Open-label RCT in mild AP (n = 60): aggressive fluids led to faster clinical improvement&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;軽症急性膵炎のオープンラベルRCT（n=60）：積極的輸液で臨床改善が早い&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;かつて主流だった大量輸液から、中等量輸液への明確な方針転換である。専門家1名（JN）が具体的な輸液速度の推奨に異論を示している。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;This marks a clear shift from aggressive to moderate fluid resuscitation. For severe disease see Q23 and Q24.&quot;,&quot;patients_ja&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;patients_en&quot;:&quot;&quot;},{&quot;q&quot;:23,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;VIII&quot;,&quot;question_en&quot;:&quot;When should nutritional support be initiated, and what is the preferred route?&quot;,&quot;question_ja&quot;:&quot;栄養療法はいつ開始すべきか、また望ましい投与経路は何か？&quot;,&quot;statements&quot;:[{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;rec&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;Early enteral tube feeding, preferably within 72 hours of onset of AP, is recommended if patients cannot tolerate oral feeding.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;経口摂取に耐えられない場合は、早期の経管経腸栄養を、できれば発症から72時間以内に開始することを推奨する。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:&quot;strong&quot;,&quot;quality&quot;:&quot;high&quot;}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;経腸栄養は静脈栄養より優先される。腸管の統合性を保ち、臓器不全、感染性膵壊死、入院期間、死亡を減らすためである。静脈栄養にはカテーテル敗血症、代謝・電解質異常、高血糖、腸管萎縮、細菌異常増殖などの問題がある。&quot;,&quot;開始時期については、入院24時間以内や48時間以内の経腸栄養開始で、死亡、臓器不全、感染、外科的介入が減るというメタ解析が複数ある。&quot;,&quot;一方、予測重症例を対象とした多施設RCTでは、早期の経鼻経腸栄養は72時間後の経口摂取開始に勝らず、オンデマンド群の多くは経管栄養を必要としなかった。まず経口摂取を試し、耐えられない場合に経管栄養へ進むのが現実的である。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;Enteral nutrition is preferred over parenteral nutrition because it maintains gut integrity and reduces organ failure, infected necrosis, length of stay and mortality. Parenteral nutrition carries risks such as catheter sepsis, metabolic and electrolyte disturbances, hyperglycemia, intestinal atrophy and bacterial overgrowth.&quot;,&quot;Regarding timing, several meta-analyses link enteral feeding started within 24 or 48 hours of admission to lower mortality, organ failure, infections and need for surgery.&quot;,&quot;However, a multicenter RCT in predicted severe AP found early nasoenteric feeding no better than an oral diet started after 72 hours, and most patients in the on-demand arm never needed tube feeding. A practical approach is to try oral intake first and move to tube feeding only if it fails.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Meta-analysis of 8 RCTs (165 patients): enteral nutrition within 24 hours lowered a combined endpoint of mortality, infected necrosis and organ failure (45% vs 19%, OR 0.44, 95% CI 0.2 to 0.96)&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;8件のRCT（165例）のメタ解析：24時間以内の経腸栄養開始で死亡、感染性膵壊死、臓器不全の複合エンドポイントが低下（45%対19%、OR 0.44、95%CI 0.2〜0.96）&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Multicenter RCT in predicted severe AP: early nasoenteric feeding not superior to oral diet after 72 hours, and 72 patients (69%) in the on-demand group tolerated oral diet&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;予測重症例の多施設RCT：早期経鼻経腸栄養は72時間後の経口摂取に勝らず、オンデマンド群の72例（69%）は経口摂取に耐えた&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Meta-analysis of 17 studies: enteral feeding before 48 hours associated with lower mortality (3.9 fold), sepsis and hospital stay&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;17研究のメタ解析：48時間以内の経腸栄養開始で死亡（3.9倍の差）、敗血症、入院期間が減少&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;RCT of appetite-guided early enteral feeding in moderately severe or severe AP: feasible and shortened hospital stay&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;中等症から重症例での食欲に基づく早期経腸栄養のRCT：実施可能で入院期間を短縮&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;「早期」の定義は研究によって24時間、48時間、72時間とばらつきがある。推奨文は72時間以内を目安としている。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;Definitions of early vary across studies (24, 48 or 72 hours). A single broad-spectrum agent is generally preferred unless Pseudomonas is suspected. Colonization is also frequent, for example after prolonged percutaneous drainage, and must be distinguished from invasive infection. The Candida colonization index has not been validated, so decisions must weigh several factors.&quot;,&quot;Antifungal therapy may be considered as an adjunct in critically ill patients who do not respond to antibiotics, drainage and necrosectomy, even with negative fungal cultures. An echinocandin is the preferred initial agent for suspected candidiasis.&quot;,&quot;Proven fungal infection requires source control by drainage or necrosectomy and drug selection by susceptibility. Treatment duration is not well defined. Prophylaxis is not indicated because it may select resistant fungi and increase mortality.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Meta-analysis of 22 studies (2151 patients with necrotizing AP): mean incidence of fungal infection 26.6%, with higher in-hospital mortality (OR 3.95, 95% CI 2.6 to 5.8)&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;22研究（壊死性膵炎2151例）のメタ解析：真菌感染の平均発生率26.6%、院内死亡が高い（OR 3.95、95%CI 2.6〜5.8）&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;推奨しないのは予防投与と経験的投与のルーチン使用であり、治療抵抗性の重症例では抗真菌薬の追加を個別に検討する。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;What is discouraged is routine prophylactic or empirical use. In refractory critically ill patients, adding an antifungal can be considered case by case.&quot;,&quot;patients_ja&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;patients_en&quot;:&quot;&quot;},{&quot;q&quot;:35,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;X&quot;,&quot;question_en&quot;:&quot;How should failure of medical conservative therapy for infected necrotizing pancreatitis be defined?&quot;,&quot;question_ja&quot;:&quot;感染性壊死性膵炎に対する内科的保存的治療の不成功はどのように定義すべきか？&quot;,&quot;statements&quot;:[{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;gps&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;Failure of medical conservative therapy should be defined if, despite the application of broad-spectrum or targeted antibiotic therapy and supportive therapy, the clinical condition of patients with suspected infected necrotizing pancreatitis does not improve (persistent fever and leukocytosis) or worsens with the development of sepsis.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;広域または標的抗菌薬治療と支持療法を行っても、感染性壊死性膵炎が疑われる患者の臨床状態が改善しない（発熱と白血球増多の持続）、または敗血症の発症により悪化する場合に、内科的保存的治療の不成功と定義する。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:null,&quot;quality&quot;:null}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;全身状態が安定した感染性膵壊死では抗菌薬を継続し、可能であれば介入を少なくとも4週間遅らせる。一方、敗血症と進行性の（多）臓器不全を伴い急速に悪化する患者もあり、この場合は介入しても死亡率が高い。&quot;,&quot;保存的治療の不成功を一定期間内に予測できる画像所見や血清マーカーは現時点でない。体温、白血球数、CRPまたはプロカルシトニン、臓器機能を定期的に評価する。&quot;,&quot;新規または持続する発熱、頻脈、白血球数の増加、プロカルシトニン上昇、臓器不全は感染の持続を示す。最大限の保存的治療でも悪化する場合は、全身状態とCTによるドレナージ可能性の評価をもとに、ドレナージやネクロセクトミーへのステップアップを判断する。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;In stable patients with infected necrosis, antibiotics should continue and intervention should be delayed for at least 4 weeks if possible. Temperature, leukocyte count, CRP or procalcitonin, and organ function should be monitored regularly.&quot;,&quot;New or persistent fever, tachycardia, rising leukocyte count, increasing procalcitonin and organ dysfunction indicate persistent infection. Clinical suspicion of, or documented, infected necrotizing pancreatitis with clinical deterioration, preferably when the necrotic collection has become walled off. ii. Prolonged symptomatic sterile walled-off necrosis, e.g., abdominal pain, gastrointestinal luminal or biliary obstruction, or nutritional failure, even without signs of infection. iii. Less common indications for intervention are pancreatic hemorrhage, and bowel ischemia and fistula.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;急性壊死性膵炎における介入（放射線学的、内視鏡的、外科的のいずれか）の一般的な適応は以下のとおりである。i. 臨床的に感染性膵壊死が疑われる、または確認され、臨床的に増悪している場合。可能であれば壊死性貯留が被包化してから行う。ii. 遷延する有症状の無菌性被包化壊死（WON）。例として腹痛、消化管内腔や胆道の閉塞、栄養障害などで、感染徴候がなくても適応となる。iii. 頻度は低いが、膵出血、腸管虚血および瘻孔も介入の適応である。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:&quot;strong&quot;,&quot;quality&quot;:&quot;low&quot;}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;壊死性膵炎の治療は保存的治療、内視鏡、経皮的手技、外科を組み合わせた多職種連携が基本である。抗菌薬に反応しない感染性膵壊死は介入の明確な適応となる。&quot;,&quot;無菌性WONでも、大きな貯留による胃排出路閉塞、膵頭部腫大による閉塞性黄疸、経口摂取不良による栄養障害、体重減少、全身倦怠の遷延などがあれば介入を考える。前向きコホートでは、こうした「持続する不調」を示す例は発症後6〜8週で介入が必要になることが多いと示唆されている。&quot;,&quot;まれな適応として出血、腸管虚血、消化管瘻があり、膵胸膜瘻や膵性腹水は非外科的介入の対象となる。感染の有無ではなく、臨床経過と症状で判断する姿勢が求められる。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;Necrotizing pancreatitis is managed by a multidisciplinary team combining conservative, endoscopic, percutaneous and surgical options. Patients with infected necrotizing pancreatitis who fail to improve clinically despite adequate antibiotic treatment and cannot undergo drainage either percutaneously or endoscopically.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;step-up approachの過程で、ネクロセクトミーは以下の場合に推奨される。i. 感受性のある抗菌薬の最適な使用と適切なドレナージ（経皮的または内視鏡的）にもかかわらず、発熱や敗血症徴候が持続する患者。ii. 適切な抗菌薬治療にもかかわらず臨床的に改善せず、経皮的にも内視鏡的にもドレナージができない感染性膵壊死患者。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:&quot;strong&quot;,&quot;quality&quot;:&quot;high&quot;}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;抗菌薬と十分なドレナージで改善しなければ、次の段階はネクロセクトミーである。膵実質壊死が50%を超える広範な貯留や固形壊死物が多い貯留では、ドレナージ単独の成功率は低い。&quot;,&quot;PCDはカテーテルのサイズアップ、追加留置、洗浄、出納の厳密な管理などを積極的に行うと成功率が上がる。感染制御の指標は解熱、プロカルシトニン、CRP、白血球数の低下、臓器不全の改善である。新規または増悪する臓器不全はstep-upの判断基準として使われてきた。&quot;,&quot;PCDで初期に改善しても、発熱が続き栄養状態が悪化する例ではネクロセクトミーの判断が難しい。患者の全身状態と栄養状態、step-up治療への耐容性を常に評価する。ネクロセクトミーは壊死が被包化し液状化する発症4週以降に行うと、境界が明瞭で出血合併症が少ない。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;If infection is not controlled by antibiotics and adequate drainage, necrosectomy is the next step. Drainage is less likely to succeed in extensive collections with more than 50% parenchymal necrosis or large amounts of solid debris.&quot;,&quot;Proactive PCD management with upsizing, extra catheters, irrigation and strict input and output monitoring improves success. Infection control is judged by resolution of fever, falling procalcitonin, CRP and leukocyte count, and reversal of organ failure. New or worsening organ failure has served as a trigger to step up.&quot;,&quot;Patients who improve initially after PCD but remain febrile and nutritionally compromised are a gray zone. Minimally invasive approaches are preferred to open approaches and recommended for necrosectomy once the (peri) pancreatic necrotic collection is walled-off. ii. Minimally invasive retroperitoneal pancreatic necrosectomy is recommended through the PCD tract in patients who have undergone percutaneous drainage using a retroperitoneal route. iii. Per-oral endoscopic necrosectomy is recommended in patients who have undergone endoscopic transluminal drainage. iv. Trans-gastric surgical necrosectomy (laparoscopic or open) is also an effective single-stage procedure ensuring wide stoma with durable internal drainage in patients with WON mainly localized to the lesser sac. v. Open debridement is rarely indicated and should be reserved for patients with predominantly solid collections and other intra-abdominal complications such as bowel fistula.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;感染性膵壊死には以下のネクロセクトミー戦略を選択すべきである。i. 膵および膵周囲の壊死性貯留が被包化した後のネクロセクトミーには、開腹より低侵襲アプローチが優先され推奨される。ii. 後腹膜経路で経皮的ドレナージを受けた患者では、PCD瘻孔を通じた低侵襲後腹膜的膵ネクロセクトミーが推奨される。iii. 内視鏡的経消化管ドレナージを受けた患者では、経口的内視鏡的ネクロセクトミーが推奨される。iv. 主に網嚢に限局するWONでは、経胃的外科的ネクロセクトミー（腹腔鏡下または開腹）も、広い吻合口により持続的な内瘻化が得られる有効な一期的手術である。v. 開腹デブリードマンの適応はまれであり、主に固形成分からなる貯留や、消化管瘻などの腹腔内合併症を伴う患者に限るべきである。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:&quot;strong&quot;,&quot;quality&quot;:&quot;high&quot;}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;開腹ネクロセクトミー、特に早期の手術は予後不良である。低侵襲ネクロセクトミーは炎症反応を抑え、新規臓器不全、集中治療の必要性、死亡率を低下させる。手技の選択は貯留の種類、数、部位、全身状態、チームの経験と資源で決まり、先行したドレナージの経路を利用するのが基本である。&quot;,&quot;左前腎傍腔経路でPCDを行った例では、瘻孔を30 Frまで拡張して内視鏡や腎盂鏡で行う後腹膜的ネクロセクトミーやVARDに進む。PCD瘻孔を使う経皮的内視鏡的ネクロセクトミー（PEN）は鎮静と局所麻酔で施行できる。VARDは他の低侵襲手技より死亡率が高い傾向がある。EUS下ドレナージ後は直接内視鏡的ネクロセクトミーを行い、全体として内視鏡アプローチが第一選択、次いで後腹膜アプローチとなる。&quot;,&quot;網嚢に限局し胃後壁を圧排するWONでは、経胃的外科的ネクロセクトミーにより一期的に壊死をほぼ除去でき、胆石例では胆嚢摘出も同時に行える。開腹ネクロセクトミーは出血や消化管瘻を伴う複雑例や緊急時に限られる。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;Open necrosectomy, especially early, carries poor outcomes. Minimally invasive necrosectomy reduces the inflammatory response, new-onset organ failure, ICU need and mortality. The technique depends on collection type, number and site, the patient&#x27;s condition and team expertise, and it usually follows the tract of the preceding drainage.&quot;,&quot;After left anterior pararenal PCD, the tract can be dilated to 30 Fr for retroperitoneal necrosectomy with an endoscope or nephroscope, or for VARD. Percutaneous endoscopic necrosectomy (PEN) through the PCD tract can be done under sedation and local anesthesia. VARD appears to carry higher mortality than other less invasive options. After EUS-guided drainage, direct endoscopic necrosectomy follows, and overall endoscopy is the method of choice followed by the retroperitoneal route.&quot;,&quot;For WON confined to the lesser sac and bulging into the posterior gastric wall, transgastric surgical necrosectomy removes nearly all necrosis in a single stage and allows simultaneous cholecystectomy in gallstone cases. Abdominal compartment syndrome (ACS) is defined as a sustained IAP of &gt;20 mmHg (with or without abdominal arterial perfusion pressure &lt;60 mmHg) that is associated with new onset or progressive organ dysfunction.&quot;,&quot;text_ja&quot;:&quot;腹腔内圧上昇（IAH）は、腹腔内圧（IAP）が12 mmHg以上に持続的または反復的に上昇した状態と定義される。腹部コンパートメント症候群（ACS）は、IAPが20 mmHgを超えて持続し（腹部灌流圧60 mmHg未満の有無を問わない）、新規発症または進行性の臓器障害を伴う状態と定義される。&quot;,&quot;strength&quot;:null,&quot;quality&quot;:null}],&quot;summary_ja&quot;:[&quot;腹腔内圧の上昇は腹腔内臓器の灌流を低下させ、腸管虚血と細菌トランスロケーションを促進しうる。&quot;,&quot;実験的重症急性膵炎では、持続する高IAPが肺障害を増悪させ、腎機能と心機能を低下させる。定義は世界腹部コンパートメント症候群学会（WSACS）の基準に基づく。&quot;],&quot;summary_en&quot;:[&quot;Raised intra-abdominal pressure reduces perfusion of abdominal organs and may contribute to intestinal ischemia and bacterial translocation.&quot;,&quot;In experimental severe AP, sustained high IAP worsens lung injury and impairs renal and cardiovascular function. Antibiotics are given only for suspected systemic infection, cholangitis or infected necrosis.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Fluid resuscitation at 1.5 to 2 times maintenance may A normal first scan does not exclude injury, and CT should be repeated after 24 to 48 hours if symptoms persist, especially if the first scan was within 12 hours of injury.&quot;,&quot;Modern CECT with multiplanar reconstruction and minimum intensity projection detects ductal injury with sensitivity and specificity above 90%. MRCP is added when CT is equivocal about ductal integrity.&quot;],&quot;evidence&quot;:[{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Multicenter study (20 centers): CT sensitivity for pancreatic injury 47% to 60%, for MPD integrity sensitivity 52% to 54% and specificity 90% to 95%&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;20施設の多施設研究：膵損傷に対する CT の感度47〜60%、主膵管の連続性評価で感度52〜54%、特異度90〜95%&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;CECT with MPR and MinIP: sensitivity and specificity &gt;90% for ductal injury&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;MPR・MinIP を用いた造影 CT：膵管損傷の感度・特異度とも90%超&quot;},{&quot;en&quot;:&quot;Single-center study: CECT and MRCP comparable for detecting pancreatic duct injury&quot;,&quot;ja&quot;:&quot;単施設研究：膵管損傷の検出能は造影 CT と MRCP で同等&quot;}],&quot;note_ja&quot;:&quot;膵損傷は grade 1〜5 に分類される（補足表16）。&quot;,&quot;note_en&quot;:&quot;Pancreatic injury is graded from 1 to 5 (Supplementary Table 16).&quot;,&quot;patients_ja&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;patients_en&quot;:&quot;&quot;},{&quot;q&quot;:73,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;XIII&quot;,&quot;question_en&quot;:&quot;When is ERCP indicated in patients with pancreatic trauma?&quot;,&quot;question_ja&quot;:&quot;膵外傷患者において ERCP はいつ適応となるか？&quot;,&quot;statements&quot;:[{&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;rec&quot;,&quot;text_en&quot;:&quot;ERCP is not recommended for diagnostic purposes in patients with AP due to trauma. Up to one third have recurrence and 10% develop CP.&quot;,&quot;The risk of progression to CP is about four times higher in men, in alcohol related disease and in recurrent AP. Smoking, genetic mutations and hypertriglyceridemia are further risk factors.&quot;,&quot;AP can be the first sign of pancreatic cancer. An updated review of 31 studies gave similar estimates and showed higher risk in severe vs mild AP (39% vs 14%), necrotizing vs non-necrotizing AP (37% vs 11%) and alcohol vs biliary etiology (28% vs 12%).&quot;,&quot;Mechanisms include islet loss from necrosis, inflammation, insulin resistance, unmasking of autoimmunity and genetic predisposition. It occurs in 10 to 75% of necrotizing pancreatitis.&quot;,&quot;These patients may need intervention, reintervention, rescue surgery or longer hospital stays. Diagnosis is often made months to years after the acute necrotic collections resolve.&quot;,&quot;External pancreatic fistula (EPF) is persistent exocrine output with fluid amylase at least 3 times the serum level, classed as low output (&lt;200 ml/day) or high output (&gt;200 ml/day), and usually follows external drainage. Residual or recurrent PFC is more common. Pancreatitis confined to the upstream gland is uncommon and hard to diagnose, and recurrence is more often due to the original etiology. const QL = {high:4,moderate:3,low:2,&quot;very low&quot;:1};

function lead(it){
  const recs = (it.statements||[]).filter(s=&gt;s.kind===&#x27;rec&#x27;);
  if (!recs.length) return {kind:&#x27;gps&#x27;};
  return recs.slice().sort((a,b)=&gt;((b.strength===&#x27;strong&#x27;)-(a.strength===&#x27;strong&#x27;))||((QL[b.quality]||0)-(QL[a.quality]||0)))[0];
}
function cls(s){ if (s.kind!==&#x27;rec&#x27;) return &#x27;s-gps&#x27;; return (s.strength===&#x27;strong&#x27;?&#x27;s-strong&#x27;:&#x27;s-cond&#x27;)+&#x27; q&#x27;+(QL[s.quality]||1); }
function kindOf(it){ const l = lead(it); return l.kind!==&#x27;rec&#x27;?&#x27;g&#x27;:(l.strength===&#x27;strong&#x27;?&#x27;s&#x27;:&#x27;c&#x27;); }
function passFilt(it){ return filt===&#x27;all&#x27; || (it.statements||[]).some(s=&gt; filt===&#x27;g&#x27;?s.kind!==&#x27;rec&#x27;:(s.kind===&#x27;rec&#x27;&amp;&amp;(filt===&#x27;s&#x27;?s.strength===&#x27;strong&#x27;:s.strength===&#x27;conditional&#x27;))); }
function fields(it){ return [pick(it,&#x27;question&#x27;),...(it.statements||[]).map(s=&gt;lang===&#x27;ja&#x27;?(s.text_ja||s.text_en):s.text_en),...(pick(it,&#x27;summary&#x27;)||[]),...(it.evidence||[]).map(e=&gt;e[lang]||e.en),pick(it,&#x27;note&#x27;),it.question_en,...(it.statements||[]).map(s=&gt;s.text_en)]; }
const words = () =&gt; term.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
function hit(it){
  if (!term) return true;
  const ws = words(); const qn = term.match(/^q?(\d{1,2})$/i);
  if (qn) return it.q===+qn[1];
  const hay = fields(it).join(&#x27; &#x27;).toLowerCase();
  return ws.every(w=&gt;hay.includes(w));
}
function mark(text){
  let h = esc(text); const ws = words().filter(w=&gt;!/^q?\d{1,2}$/.test(w));
  ws.forEach(w=&gt;{ const e = esc(w).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,&#x27;\\$&amp;&#x27;); h = h.replace(new RegExp(&#x27;(&#x27;+e+&#x27;)(?![^&lt;]*&gt;)&#x27;,&#x27;gi&#x27;),&#x27;&lt;mark&gt;$1&lt;/mark&gt;&#x27;); });
  return h;
}
function snippet(it){
  const ws = words(); if (!ws.length) return &#x27;&#x27;;
  const q = pick(it,&#x27;question&#x27;).toLowerCase();
  if (ws.every(w=&gt;q.includes(w))) return &#x27;&#x27;;
  const f = fields(it).slice(1).find(t=&gt;t &amp;&amp; ws.some(w=&gt;String(t).toLowerCase().includes(w)));
  if (!f) return &#x27;&#x27;;
  const t = String(f), k = t.toLowerCase().indexOf(ws.find(w=&gt;t.toLowerCase().includes(w)));
  const a = Math.max(0,k-30), b = Math.min(t.length,k+70);
  return (a?&#x27;…&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;)+t.slice(a,b)+(b&lt;t.length?&#x27;…&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;);
}

  root.querySelectorAll(&#x27;[data-t]&#x27;).forEach(el=&gt;{ el.textContent = L(el.dataset.t); });
  root.querySelectorAll(&#x27;.lang button&#x27;).forEach(b=&gt;b.setAttribute(&#x27;aria-pressed&#x27;, String(b.dataset.lang===lang)));
  const n = k =&gt; byQ.filter(it=&gt;kindOf(it)===k).length;
  $(&#x27;tally&#x27;).innerHTML = [[byQ.length,L(&#x27;tq&#x27;)],[n(&#x27;s&#x27;),L(&#x27;ts&#x27;)],[n(&#x27;c&#x27;),L(&#x27;tc&#x27;)],[n(&#x27;g&#x27;),L(&#x27;tg&#x27;)]].map(([v,l])=&gt;`&lt;div&gt;&lt;b&gt;${v}&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;${esc(l)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;);
  $(&#x27;q&#x27;).placeholder = L(&#x27;ph&#x27;);
  $(&#x27;chips&#x27;).innerHTML = [[&#x27;all&#x27;,&#x27;fAll&#x27;],[&#x27;s&#x27;,&#x27;fS&#x27;],[&#x27;c&#x27;,&#x27;fC&#x27;],[&#x27;g&#x27;,&#x27;fG&#x27;]].map(([k,l])=&gt;`&lt;button type=&quot;button&quot; class=&quot;chip&quot; data-f=&quot;${k}&quot; aria-pressed=&quot;${filt===k}&quot;&gt;${esc(L(l))}&lt;/button&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;);
  $(&#x27;foot&#x27;).innerHTML = L(&#x27;foot&#x27;).map(p=&gt;`&lt;p&gt;${p}&lt;/p&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;);
  $(&#x27;prev&#x27;).textContent = &#x27;← &#x27;+L(&#x27;prev&#x27;); $(&#x27;next&#x27;).textContent = L(&#x27;next&#x27;)+&#x27; →&#x27;;
  $(&#x27;close&#x27;).setAttribute(&#x27;aria-label&#x27;, L(&#x27;close&#x27;));
}
const legendHTML = () =&gt; `&lt;div class=&quot;legend&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;i style=&quot;background:var(--q3)&quot;&gt;&lt;/i&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;lgS&#x27;))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;i style=&quot;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 3px var(--q3)&quot;&gt;&lt;/i&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;lgC&#x27;))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;i class=&quot;s-gps&quot;&gt;&lt;/i&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;lgG&#x27;))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;i style=&quot;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--q1),var(--q2),var(--q3),var(--q4))&quot;&gt;&lt;/i&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;lgQ&#x27;))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`;
function cardHTML(it, withSnip){
  const tags = [...new Set((it.statements||[]).map(st=&gt;st.kind===&#x27;rec&#x27;?`${L(st.strength)} · ${L(&#x27;q&#x27;)[st.quality]||&#x27;&#x27;}`:L(&#x27;tg&#x27;)))];
  const sn = withSnip ? snippet(it) : &#x27;&#x27;;
  return `&lt;button type=&quot;button&quot; class=&quot;card&quot; data-q=&quot;${it.q}&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;cell ${cls(lead(it))}&quot; aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;${it.q}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;p&gt;${withSnip?mark(pick(it,&#x27;question&#x27;)):esc(pick(it,&#x27;question&#x27;))}&lt;/p&gt;${sn?`&lt;p class=&quot;snip&quot;&gt;${mark(sn)}&lt;/p&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;span class=&quot;tags&quot;&gt;${tags.map(t=&gt;`&lt;span&gt;${esc(t)}&lt;/span&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`;
}
function render(){
  let html = &#x27;&#x27;, count = 0;
  if (term){
    const items = byQ.filter(it=&gt;hit(it)&amp;&amp;passFilt(it)); count = items.length;
    html += `&lt;div class=&quot;homeh&quot;&gt;&lt;h2&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;res&#x27;))}&lt;/h2&gt;${legendHTML()}&lt;/div&gt;`;
    DOMAINS.forEach(d=&gt;{
      const its = items.filter(it=&gt;it.q&gt;=d[1]&amp;&amp;it.q&lt;=d[2]); if (!its.length) return;
      html += `&lt;section class=&quot;dom&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;domh&quot;&gt;&lt;em&gt;${d[0]}&lt;/em&gt;&lt;h3&gt;${esc(dName(d))}&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;grid&quot;&gt;${its.map(it=&gt;cardHTML(it,true)).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;`;
    });
    if (!items.length) html += `&lt;p class=&quot;empty&quot;&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;none&#x27;))}&lt;/p&gt;`;
  } else if (view.v===&#x27;dom&#x27;){
    const di = DOMAINS.findIndex(d=&gt;d[0]===view.d), d = DOMAINS[di];
    const items = byQ.filter(it=&gt;it.q&gt;=d[1]&amp;&amp;it.q&lt;=d[2]&amp;&amp;passFilt(it)); count = items.length;
    html += `&lt;div class=&quot;crumb&quot;&gt;&lt;button type=&quot;button&quot; class=&quot;back&quot; data-go=&quot;home&quot;&gt;← ${esc(L(&#x27;back&#x27;))}&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`;
    html += `&lt;section class=&quot;dom&quot; style=&quot;padding-top:12px&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;domh&quot;&gt;&lt;em&gt;${d[0]}&lt;/em&gt;&lt;h3&gt;${esc(dName(d))}&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`;
    let lastSub = null, open = false;
    items.forEach(it=&gt;{
      const s = subOf(it.q);
      if (!open || s!==lastSub){ if (open) html += &#x27;&lt;/div&gt;&#x27;; if (s) html += `&lt;p class=&quot;sub&quot;&gt;${esc(lang===&#x27;ja&#x27;?s[2]:s[3])}&lt;/p&gt;`; html += &#x27;&lt;div class=&quot;grid&quot;&gt;&#x27;; open = true; lastSub = s; }
      html += cardHTML(it,false);
    });
    if (open) html += &#x27;&lt;/div&gt;&#x27;;
    if (!items.length) html += `&lt;p class=&quot;empty&quot;&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;none&#x27;))}&lt;/p&gt;`;
    html += &#x27;&lt;/section&gt;&#x27;;
    const pd = DOMAINS[di-1], nd = DOMAINS[di+1];
    html += `&lt;div class=&quot;dnav&quot;&gt;${pd?`&lt;button type=&quot;button&quot; data-go=&quot;${pd[0]}&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;← ${esc(L(&#x27;pd&#x27;))}&lt;/span&gt;${pd[0]} ${esc(dShort(pd))}&lt;/button&gt;`:&#x27;&lt;i&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&#x27;}${nd?`&lt;button type=&quot;button&quot; class=&quot;nx&quot; data-go=&quot;${nd[0]}&quot;&gt;&lt;span&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;nd&#x27;))} →&lt;/span&gt;${nd[0]} ${esc(dShort(nd))}&lt;/button&gt;`:&#x27;&#x27;}&lt;/div&gt;`;
  } else {
    count = byQ.filter(passFilt).length;
    html += `&lt;div class=&quot;homeh&quot;&gt;&lt;h2&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;maph&#x27;))}&lt;/h2&gt;${legendHTML()}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;panels&quot;&gt;`;
    html += DOMAINS.map(d=&gt;{
      const its = byQ.filter(it=&gt;it.q&gt;=d[1]&amp;&amp;it.q&lt;=d[2]);
      const nOk = its.filter(passFilt).length;
      return `&lt;button type=&quot;button&quot; class=&quot;panel&quot; data-go=&quot;${d[0]}&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;pn&quot;&gt;&lt;em&gt;${d[0]}&lt;/em&gt;&lt;small&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;cqn&#x27;)(filt===&#x27;all&#x27;?its.length:nOk))}&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;full&quot;&gt;${esc(dName(d))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;short&quot;&gt;${esc(dShort(d))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;mini&quot; aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;${its.map(it=&gt;`&lt;i class=&quot;${cls(lead(it))}${passFilt(it)?&#x27;&#x27;:&#x27; dim&#x27;}&quot;&gt;&lt;/i&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;`;
    }).join(&#x27;&#x27;) + &#x27;&lt;/div&gt;&#x27;;
  }
  $(&#x27;list&#x27;).innerHTML = html;
  $(&#x27;count&#x27;).textContent = L(&#x27;cnt&#x27;)(count);
}

function stmtHTML(s){
  const isRec = s.kind===&#x27;rec&#x27;;
  const qn = QL[s.quality]||0;
  const meta = isRec ? `&lt;span class=&quot;pill str&quot;&gt;${esc(L(s.strength))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;ql&#x27;))}：${esc(L(&#x27;q&#x27;)[s.quality]||&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;meter&quot; aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;${[1,2,3,4].map(i=&gt;`&lt;i class=&quot;${i&lt;=qn?&#x27;f&#x27;:&#x27;&#x27;}&quot;&gt;&lt;/i&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/span&gt;` : &#x27;&#x27;;
  const brk = t =&gt; String(t||&#x27;&#x27;).replace(/\s+(?=(?:\(?(?:i{1,3}|iv|v|vi)[.)])\s)/g,&#x27;\n&#x27;);
  const txt = brk(lang===&#x27;ja&#x27; ? (s.text_ja||s.text_en) : s.text_en);
  const orig = lang===&#x27;ja&#x27; &amp;&amp; s.text_ja ? `&lt;details class=&quot;orig&quot;&gt;&lt;summary&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;orig&#x27;))}&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;p&gt;${esc(brk(s.text_en))}&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/details&gt;` : &#x27;&#x27;;
  return `&lt;div class=&quot;stmt ${isRec?&#x27;q&#x27;+(qn||1):&#x27;&#x27;}&quot;&gt;&lt;div class=&quot;k&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;pill kind&quot;&gt;${esc(isRec?L(&#x27;rec&#x27;):L(&#x27;gps&#x27;))}&lt;/span&gt;${meta}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;${esc(txt)}&lt;/p&gt;${orig}&lt;/div&gt;`;
}
function fillSheet(idx){
  cur = idx; const it = byQ[idx]; const d = domOf(it.q);
  $(&#x27;shmeta&#x27;).textContent = `${d[0]} · ${dName(d)}`;
  const sum = pick(it,&#x27;summary&#x27;), ev = it.evidence||[], note = pick(it,&#x27;note&#x27;), pt = pick(it,&#x27;patients&#x27;);
  $(&#x27;shbody&#x27;).innerHTML =
    `&lt;div class=&quot;sh-q&quot;&gt;&lt;span class=&quot;cell ${cls(lead(it))}&quot;&gt;${it.q}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h4 id=&quot;shq&quot;&gt;${esc(pick(it,&#x27;question&#x27;))}&lt;/h4&gt;&lt;/div&gt;` +
    (it.statements||[]).map(stmtHTML).join(&#x27;&#x27;) +

    (ev.length ? `&lt;h5&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;ev&#x27;))}&lt;/h5&gt;&lt;ul class=&quot;ev&quot;&gt;${ev.map(e=&gt;`&lt;li&gt;${esc(e[lang]||e.en)}&lt;/li&gt;`).join(&#x27;&#x27;)}&lt;/ul&gt;` : &#x27;&#x27;) +
    (note ? `&lt;div class=&quot;note&quot;&gt;&lt;b&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;note&#x27;))}&lt;/b&gt;${esc(note)}&lt;/div&gt;` : &#x27;&#x27;) +
    (pt ? `&lt;div class=&quot;note&quot;&gt;&lt;b&gt;${esc(L(&#x27;pt&#x27;))}&lt;/b&gt;${esc(pt)}&lt;/div&gt;` : &#x27;&#x27;);
  $(&#x27;shbody&#x27;).scrollTop = 0;
  $(&#x27;prev&#x27;).disabled = idx===0; $(&#x27;next&#x27;).disabled = idx===byQ.length-1;
}

function showSheet(){

function hideSheet(){
  const sh = $(&#x27;sheet&#x27;), sc = $(&#x27;scrim&#x27;);
  if (sh.hidden) return;
  if (EMBED){ sh.hidden = true; sh.classList.remove(&#x27;on&#x27;); $(&#x27;list&#x27;).hidden = false; const c = root.querySelector(`.card[data-q=&quot;${byQ[cur]?.q}&quot;]`); tell({appScroll:c?yOf(c)-80:yOf($(&#x27;main&#x27;))}); return; }

}
function toTop(){ if (EMBED){ if (inApp) tell({appScroll:yOf($(&#x27;main&#x27;))}); return; } const r = $(&#x27;main&#x27;).getBoundingClientRect(); if (r.top &lt; 0 || (view.v===&#x27;dom&#x27; &amp;&amp; inApp)) $(&#x27;main&#x27;).scrollIntoView({block:&#x27;start&#x27;}); }

// routing: &#x27;&#x27; home, #d-XI domain, #q42 question (shown over its domain)
function route(){
  const h = location.hash;
  let m;
  if ((m = h.match(/^#q(\d+)$/))){
    const i = byQ.findIndex(it=&gt;it.q===+m[1]);
    if (i&gt;=0){ if (!term &amp;&amp; view.v!==&#x27;dom&#x27;){ view = {v:&#x27;dom&#x27;, d:domOf(byQ[i].q)[0]}; render(); } fillSheet(i); showSheet(); (inApp &amp;&amp; tell({appHash:location.hash})); return; }
  }
  hideSheet();
  const nv = (m = h.match(/^#d-([IVX]+)$/)) &amp;&amp; DOMAINS.some(d=&gt;d[0]===m[1]) ? {v:&#x27;dom&#x27;, d:m[1]} : {v:&#x27;home&#x27;};
  const changed = nv.v!==view.v || nv.d!==view.d;
  view = nv;
  if (changed){ if (term){ term=&#x27;&#x27;; $(&#x27;q&#x27;).value=&#x27;&#x27;; } render(); toTop(); }
  (inApp &amp;&amp; tell({appHash:location.hash}));
}

  const typing = e.target.closest &amp;&amp; e.target.closest(&#x27;input,textarea,[contenteditable]&#x27;);
  if (!$(&#x27;sheet&#x27;).hidden){
    if (e.key===&#x27;Escape&#x27;) closeSheet();

  }
  if (e.key===&#x27;/&#x27; &amp;&amp; !typing){ e.preventDefault(); $(&#x27;q&#x27;).focus(); }
